====================================================================================================
QUESTION: If the employee leaves employment and wants to continue coverage without a medical exam, which option is appropriate — conversion or portability?
CATEGORY: CAT4_MULTIHOP_REASONING
EXPECTED ANSWER: Both conversion and portability are available without evidence of insurability. Conversion provides an individual whole life policy up to $200,000; portability continues the same group amount at age-banded rates.
EVIDENCE: 5.1 If your group coverage terminates for any reason except non-payment of premium, you may convert to an individual whole life policy without evidence of insurability. / 6.5 Evidence of insurability is NOT required for portability.
====================================================================================================
